2026-10-25T14:17:02.7663005Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Gate the one-row Decode norm/quant at rounding level and serve iteration 2 on image 40197369083

Run 40197369083 built the iteration-2 image, but Slurm job 26163 stopped in
the provisioning gate before any server started. The first fatal error was
AssertionError ('decode_norm_quant_127', 'fp8', 'not bitwise equal') in
check_decode_add_rmsnorm_group_quant. That gate required bitwise equality
against flashinfer fused_add_rmsnorm plus per-token group quant. The existing
two-row fusion has never met that: in the same log the Prefill screen
recorded 512 differing FP8 codes at 127 rows (478 at 128, 586 at 144) with
identical scales, and it passes only under its rounding-level bounds. Rows 0
and 1 matched only because row 0 is zeroed. The one-row Decode layout keeps
the same per-row arithmetic, so the strict gate rejected arithmetic that is
already served. The task rules allow FP summation-order differences.

Move the Prefill comparison to a module-level compare_norm_quant and use it
for the Decode one-row gate too. The residual stays bitwise, the scale may
move by at most one BF16 ulp, and each dequantized FP8 value by at most one
E4M3 step. The number of differing codes is printed for every row count and
graph replay.

Pin configs/firmus-framework.json to the image this run built (SHA256
1f247668..., built from cbca2b3), so this commit reuses it without a
rebuild. Only the gate script and the pin change; the kernels, overlays and
candidate tables are the ones cbca2b3 built.

Selector results from the stopped run, recorded for the next iteration:
- Decode dense: DG1 SwapAB stream-K measured 107.4 us for QKV and 118.1 us
  for O at 176 rows, against stock at 24.2 us and 17.4 us. It is rejected.
  The selector keeps dg-128-144-5-1-0 for QKV (23.4 us) and stock for O.
- Decode FA3: the merge visibility correction did not change the failure.
  All merge candidates still fail verify_fp8_codes at relative L2 0.7315,
  so the defect is not L1 visibility. The winner is p128split2-s3-fp8 at
  71.6 us (p128split2-fp8 72.0 us, control96 93.7 us).
Both stay in the image and are rejected inside the job. Remove them at the
next rebuild.
